[AAPL_Q4_2024] iPhone revenue was $46.2 billion, up 6% year over year and a September quarter record in total and across several markets, including the U.S., the Middle East, Korea, and South Asia.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] We continue to see high levels of customer satisfaction for the iPhone 15 family with 451 Research recently measuring it at 98% in the U.S. Mac revenue was $7.7 billion, up 2% year over year, driven by the strength in MacBook Air.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] And in the latest reports from 451 Research, customer satisfaction was 95% in the U.S. iPad generated $7 billion in revenue, up 8% year over year.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] Also, customer satisfaction was recently measured at 97% in the U.S. Wearables, Home, and Accessories revenue was $9 billion, down 3% year over year.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] And the latest reports from 451 Research indicated customer satisfaction of 96% for Watch in the U.S. Our Services revenue reached an all-time record of $25 billion, growing 12% year over year.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] Today, Apple is reporting revenue of $94.9 billion, a September quarter record and up 6% from a year ago.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] iPhone grew in every geographic segment, marking a new September quarter revenue record for the category, and services set an all-time revenue record, up 12% year over year.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] iPhone revenues set a September quarter record of $46.2 billion, up 6% from a year ago with growth in every geographic segment.

[AAPL_Q4_2024] iPad revenue was $7 billion, 8% higher year over year.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] In Wearables, Home, and Accessories, revenue was $9 billion, down 3% from a year ago.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] As I mentioned earlier, Services achieved an all-time revenue record of $25 billion, up 12% from a year ago and with all-time revenue records across most of our categories.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] We are very pleased to report a new September quarter revenue record of $94.9 billion, up 6% year over year.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] We grew in the vast majority of the markets we track and achieved September quarter revenue records in the Americas, Europe, and rest of Asia Pacific.
[AAPL_Q4_2024] Products revenue was $70 billion, up 4% year over year, driven by growth in iPhone, iPad and Mac.
